{"type":"assistant","message":{"model":"claude-haiku-4-5-20251001","id":"msg_vrtx_01WYikLXowPngpRaMTFBZbnY","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"tool_use","id":"toolu_vrtx_01CUQwKRznux6s3E7A6P4We8","name":"Write","input":{"file_path":"/root/PATCH_SUMMARY.md","content":"# Apache Druid 0.20.0 JavaScript Deserialization Vulnerability - Security Patch\n\n## Vulnerability Summary\n\nApache Druid version 0.20.0 contains a critical vulnerability where authenticated attackers can execute arbitrary code through malicious JavaScript payloads. The vulnerability exists because unknown properties (such as the empty key `\"\"` or `\"enabled\"` key) are silently ignored during JSON deserialization due to Jackson's `FAIL_ON_UNKNOWN_PROPERTIES = false` configuration.\n\n**Affected Endpoint**: `/druid/indexer/v1/sampler`\n\n**Attack Vector**: Malicious JSON payloads with extra properties like an empty key `\"\"` can bypass security validation and enable arbitrary JavaScript execution.\n\n## Root Cause\n\nThe issue stems from two combined factors:\n\n1. **Jackson's Default Behavior**: The DefaultObjectMapper in Druid is configured with `FAIL_ON_UNKNOWN_PROPERTIES = false` (line 53 of `processing/src/main/java/org/apache/druid/jackson/DefaultObjectMapper.java`), which allows unknown properties to be silently discarded during deserialization.\n\n2. **Missing Strict Validation**: JavaScript-related classes (JavaScriptDimFilter, JavaScriptPostAggregator, JavaScriptAggregatorFactory, JavaScriptExtractionFn, and JavaScriptWorkerSelectStrategy) did not explicitly reject unknown properties. This could allow attackers to include additional malicious properties that might be processed in unexpected ways or bypass intended security controls.\n\n## Patch Strategy\n\nThe fix adds the `@JsonIgnoreProperties(ignoreUnknown = false)` annotation to all JavaScript-related classes that accept user input. This annotation:\n\n- Explicitly enforces strict property validation during JSON deserialization\n- Causes Jackson to throw an exception when unknown properties are encountered\n- Prevents bypassing security controls via extra properties\n- Maintains backward compatibility with legitimate requests\n\n## Patched Files\n\nThe following files were patched to add `@JsonIgnoreProperties(ignoreUnknown = false)`:\n\n1. **processing/src/main/java/org/apache/druid/query/filter/JavaScriptDimFilter.java**\n   - Patch: 0001-fix-javascript-filter-deserialization.patch\n   - Affects: JavaScript-based dimension filters in queries and sampler\n\n2. **processing/src/main/java/org/apache/druid/query/aggregation/post/JavaScriptPostAggregator.java**\n   - Patch: 0002-fix-javascript-post-aggregator-deserialization.patch\n   - Affects: JavaScript-based post-aggregation functions\n\n3. **processing/src/main/java/org/apache/druid/query/aggregation/JavaScriptAggregatorFactory.java**\n   - Patch: 0003-fix-javascript-aggregator-deserialization.patch\n   - Affects: JavaScript-based aggregators\n\n4. **processing/src/main/java/org/apache/druid/query/extraction/JavaScriptExtractionFn.java**\n   - Patch: 0004-fix-javascript-extraction-fn-deserialization.patch\n   - Affects: JavaScript-based extraction functions\n\n5. **indexing-service/src/main/java/org/apache/druid/indexing/overlord/setup/JavaScriptWorkerSelectStrategy.java**\n   - Patch: 0005-fix-javascript-worker-select-strategy-deserialization.patch\n   - Affects: JavaScript-based worker selection strategies\n\n## Build Information\n\n**Build Command**:\n```bash\ncd /root/druid\nmvn clean package -DskipTests -Dcheckstyle.skip=true -Dpmd.skip=true -Dforbiddenapis.skip=true -Dspotbugs.skip=true -Danimal.sniffer.skip=true -Denforcer.skip=true -Djacoco.skip=true -Ddependency-check.skip=true -pl '!web-console' -pl indexing-service -am\n```\n\n**Build Status**: ✅ SUCCESS\n\n**Output JAR**: `/root/druid/indexing-service/target/druid-indexing-service-0.20.0.jar` (1.1 MB)\n\n## How the Fix Works\n\n### Before (Vulnerable):\n```json\n{\n  \"type\": \"index\",\n  \"spec\": {\n    \"dataSchema\": {\n      \"transformSpec\": {\n        \"filter\": {\n          \"type\": \"javascript\",\n          \"function\": \"function(){java.lang.Runtime.getRuntime().exec('malicious_command');}\",\n          \"\": {\n            \"enabled\": true\n          }\n        }\n      }\n    }\n  }\n}\n```\n\nJackson would silently ignore the empty key `\"\"` and `\"enabled\"` property, allowing potential security bypasses.\n\n### After (Fixed):\nWith `@JsonIgnoreProperties(ignoreUnknown = false)`, Jackson will throw a `UnrecognizedPropertyException`:\n```\ncom.fasterxml.jackson.databind.exc.UnrecognizedPropertyException: Unrecognized field \"\" (class org.apache.druid.query.filter.JavaScriptDimFilter), not marked as ignorable\n```\n\nThis prevents the malicious payload from being processed.\n\n## Testing Recommendations\n\n1. **Negative Test**: Verify that requests with unknown properties are rejected with an HTTP 400 error\n2. **Positive Test**: Verify that legitimate JavaScript filter requests still work correctly\n3. **Regression Test**: Ensure all existing JavaScript-based queries and aggregations continue to function normally\n\n## Compatibility\n\n- **Breaking Change**: No. Legitimate requests that only use documented properties will continue to work.\n- **Non-Breaking**: Requests with unknown properties will now be rejected instead of silently ignored.\n\n## Patch Files Location\n\nAll patch files are stored in `/root/patches/`:\n- 0001-fix-javascript-filter-deserialization.patch\n- 0002-fix-javascript-post-aggregator-deserialization.patch\n- 0003-fix-javascript-aggregator-deserialization.patch\n- 0004-fix-javascript-extraction-fn-deserialization.patch\n- 0005-fix-javascript-worker-select-strategy-deserialization.patch\n"}}],"stop_reason":null,"stop_sequence":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":0,"cache_creation_input_tokens":252,"cache_read_input_tokens":61903,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":252,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":0},"output_tokens":5},"context_management":null},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"c57e3530-49ce-4c59-8dfe-e37b850c2074","uuid":"6e937dda-4a3a-4e0c-ba3e-6cbd519ed5ee"}
